2.   COMMITTED TO EXCELLENCE, DEDICATED TO THE WELL-BEING OF PEOPLE
     2.1.   R&D Innovation

            As one of the pioneers and leading developers of ADCs, Kelun-Biotech consistently regards the research
            and development of innovative drugs and the enhancement of human health as its corporate mission. The
            Company has established a comprehensive research and development innovation system, possessing three
            independently developed technology platforms, including the ADC and novel DC platform OptiDC™, the
            biologics platform and the small molecule platform.

            According to Frost & Sullivan, we are one of the first biopharmaceutical companies in China, and one of the
            few globally, to establish a developed integrated ADC and novel DC platform. Our ADC and novel DC platform,
            OptiDC™, is supported by three capability pillars: in-depth knowledge of biological targets and diseases,
            tested and validated DC design and development know-how, and a toolbox of core DC components. With
            a developed toolbox of core DC components, the Company has the versatility to engineer customized
            ADCs and novel DCs optimized for different biological targets to address medical needs in a broad range of
            indications. Our biologics platform enables the creation and refinement of cutting-edge mAb/bsAb medicines
            across the entire drug development lifecycle – from target biology to clinical-grade biologics. By integrating
            advanced technologies and workflows, including single B cell screening, next-generation sequencing, and high-
            throughput screening and analysis, the platform accelerates the generation of innovative antibodies with desired
            properties. Our small molecule platform is driven by the integration of medicinal chemistry, CADD (computer-
            aided drug design) and AIDD technologies, such as molecular docking, pharmacophore modeling, FEP
            (free energy perturbation) calculations, ADMET (absorption, distribution, metabolism, elimination and toxicity)
            prediction, and de novo molecule generation.

            The Company has established a diversified research and development pipeline centered on oncology while also
            addressing non-oncological diseases, encompassing multiple cutting-edge technology areas such as ADCs,
            monoclonal antibodies, and small molecule drugs. The Company’s research and development pipeline targets
            the world’s most common or hardest-to-treat tumors, such as breast cancer (BC), non-small cell lung cancer
            (NSCLC), gastrointestinal cancers (GI), gynecological tumors, and genitourinary (GU) cancers as well as non-
            tumor diseases and conditions affecting a large and underserved patient population. By the end of 2025, we
            had over 30 drug candidates in development, of which 4 had been approved for marketing, 1 was in the NDA
            stage, and more than 10 were in the clinical stage.

            Additionally, as of the end of the Reporting Period, among the four innovative drugs from Kelun-Biotech that
            have been approved for marketing and successfully commercialized, three were included for the first time in the
            2025 edition of the National Reimbursement Drug List (NRDL), as detailed below:

            •      Sac-TMT (Sacituzumab Tirumotecan for Injection) (Trade Name: 佳泰萊®): A TROP2-targeted ADC
                   drug, initially approved in November 2024 for locally advanced/metastatic triple-negative breast cancer;
                   in March and October 2025, additional indications were approved for 2L and 3L EGFR mutation-
                   positive non-squamous non-small cell lung cancer, of which the indications for locally advanced/
                   metastatic triple-negative breast cancer and for 3L EGFR mutation-positive non-squamous non-small
                   cell lung cancer have been included in the NRDL.

            •      Tagrisimab Injection (Trade Name: 科泰萊®): An innovative PD-L1 inhibitor, approved in December
                   2024 for recurrent/metastatic nasopharyngeal carcinoma; in January 2025, an additional indication was
                   approved for first-line treatment of nasopharyngeal carcinoma in combination with chemotherapy, both
                   of which have been included in the NRDL.



                                                                                                 Annual Report 2025 133
ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND GOVERNANCE REPORT




            •       Cetuximab N01 Injection (Trade Name: 達泰萊®): An EGFR monoclonal antibody biologic, approved in
                    February 2025 for the indication – RAS wild-type metastatic colorectal cancer, which has been included
                    in the NRDL.

            •       Trastuzumab Botidotin for Injection (Trade Name: 舒泰萊®): A HER2-targeted ADC drug, approved in
                    October 2025 for unresectable/metastatic HER2-positive adult breast cancer previously treated with at
                    least one anti-HER2 therapy, marking it as the first domestically produced HER2 ADC drug in China to
                    broadly cover second-line and beyond indications for this condition.

            The Company’s strong R&D innovation capability is inseparable from a high-level R&D team and continuously
            increasing R&D investment. By the end of 2025, the Company has 925 R&D members (full-time employees). In
            2025, the Company’s R&D investment reached RMB1,319.67 million, accounting for 64.12% of the operating
            income.

     2.2.   Quality Responsibility

            Kelun-Biotech focuses on the research, development, production, and commercialization of innovative drugs,
            prioritizing product quality and patient safety as its foremost operational principles. Adhering to its established
            quality policy, the Company collaborates with partners to forge a supply chain quality responsibility community,
            implementing stringent quality control across all stages to deliver safe and effective innovative drugs and fulfill its
            commitment to safeguarding life and health.

            Product Quality Management

            Quality Management System

            The Company has established a comprehensive product lifecycle quality management system covering
            all stages of research and development, production, and commercialization, strictly adhering to a series of
            pharmaceutical quality management and compliance regulations (GxP) in China, the EU, and the US, as well
            as guidelines from the International Council for Harmonisation of Technical Requirements for Pharmaceuticals
            for Human Use (ICH). In 2025, the Company continued to advance the optimization of management system
            documents and further refine the quality management systems for research and development, clinical studies,
            and production.

            The Company places quality assurance at the core of its operations. In 2025, we successfully passed seven
            external audits/inspections, including licensing inspection and GMP inspection for products entrusted for
            manufacturing (including split manufacturing) in China, post-marketing drug supervision inspection, audits by
            the EU Qualified Person (EU QP) and MNC customer audit. All commercial-scale production facilities of the
            Company in operation have 100% passed the compliance inspection of the GMP in China. Additionally, we
            conducted comprehensive self-inspections of quality systems, with no critical deficiencies identified, and all
            related issues have been fully rectified as planned.

            In terms of setting and managing quality objectives, we have established a multi-level quality objective
            performance management system for the Company, departments, workshops, and employees, further
            strengthening the assessment standards compared to the year 2024. For example, the Company has raised
            its target requirements for the timely closure rate of quality incidents and the timely completion rate of corrective
            actions from 90% to 92%. Meanwhile, the Company has incorporated quality objectives’ veto and deduction
            items into the performance assessment criteria for all employees to continuously enhance the awareness of
            quality among all staff, ensuring that product quality consistently remains at a high standard level.

Product Quality Control

The Company adheres to the philosophy of “rooted in stringent control of drug quality and grounded in ensuring
the safety and efficacy of pharmaceuticals”, implementing rigorous quality control measures at every stage of
product research, development, and production.

During the product development stage, the Company formulates the target product quality profile (QTPP) based
on the principle of “Quality by Design”, clearly defining the expected product quality attributes. Subsequently,
we determine the criticality of quality attributes based on data assessment from pharmaceutical, nonclinical and
clinical studies, and use risk control tools to identify material attributes (CMA) and process parameters affecting
critical product quality attributes (CQA). The product quality control strategy is gradually determined and
implemented through process study, characterization and validation, and the control strategy is continuously
optimized and updated as the study process progresses to ensure product quality.


                     The Company’s laboratory has been accredited by
           Case:     the China National Accreditation Service for Conformity Assessment (CNAS)


     In the product manufacturing process, the Company implements comprehensive quality control
     and management. Relying on the comprehensive and technologically advanced quality control
     laboratory, the Company carries out the release testing of raw materials, intermediate products
     and products, in-process control, validation, stability studies, and monitoring related to utility
     systems and environment to ensure the safety and quality control of released products at all
     stages. For critical testing instruments, the company adopts a one-in-use, one-in-reserve
     configuration to ensure the continuous and stable conduct of inspection work. The Company’ s
     laboratory has been accredited by the China National Accreditation Service for Conformity
     Assessment (CNAS) (Registration number: CNAS L25062).




During the Reporting Period, we continued to strengthen quality management measures from multiple
dimensions. We established a data reliability system across the entire R&D lifecycle and enhanced oversight
through CRO audits and inspections. Simultaneously, we continuously optimized production system
documentation and key processes, improved the linkage mechanism between complaint handling and
pharmacovigilance, and conducted tiered self-inspections and regulatory benchmarking. Additionally, we
assisted contracted parties in elevating compliance standards, implemented differentiated supervision
mechanisms, and carried out audits and risk assessments based on product stages, achieving a dual
enhancement in risk prevention and regulatory capabilities.

Clinical Quality Management

As a clinical trial sponsor, the Company is firmly committed to safeguarding the rights, safety and wellbeing of
clinical trial participants in a bid to ensure compliance with the ethical principles of the Declaration of Helsinki
and reliability of clinical trial data and results. The Company strictly adheres to the Drug Administration Law
of the People’s Republic of China, the Regulations for Implementation of the Drug Administration Law of the
People’s Republic of China, the Provisions for Drug Registration, the Good Clinical Practice for Drug Clinical
Trials, the ICH E6 Good Clinical Practice – Technical Guideline, the Declaration of Helsinki and other laws and
regulations. A risk-based clinical trial quality management system commensurate with the risks of clinical trials
has been established, and written standard operating procedures covering key processes from clinical trial
planning to reporting have been developed. During the Reporting Period, we continued to optimize processes,
revise system documents and enhance operational feasibility across multiple areas including clinical operations,
medical writing, quality assurance and quality control, data management, biostatistics and pharmacovigilance,
in a bid to avoid unnecessary complexity, procedures, and data collection and further support the execution of
international projects.

The Company conducts clinical quality management in the form of a “project management system”, equipping
each clinical trial project with a project management team. During the Reporting Period, with a focus on
improving quality and efficiency, we continuously optimized our quality assurance system by emphasizing
key areas such as reducing third-party audit costs, conducting training on issues identified in project audits,
optimising deviation management, and continuously cultivating a quality culture. For example, we actively
promoted internalization of audits for clinical trial projects to effectively reduce the frequency and costs
associated with third-party audits, regularly conducted specialized training on audit-identified issues, and
strengthened timely deviation reporting and conducted in-depth root cause analysis to ensure the adequacy
and effectiveness of corrective measures, achieving a 100% completion rate for deviation rectifications.

The Company purchases Drug Clinical Trial Insurance for each clinical trial participant, fully ensuring the rights,
safety and well-being of the participants. During the Reporting Period, we revised the Clinical Trial Liability
Insurance Management Procedures, optimized the processes for insurance application and policy issuance,
and further improved claims settlement efficiency.

To continuously cultivate a quality culture and enhance the quality awareness and capabilities of all staff involved
in clinical research, during the Reporting Period, focusing on themes such as the safety, rights, and well-being
of trial participants and data security and reliability, we organized four specialized regulatory training sessions,
reaching over 500 participants with a pass rate of 99.6% in assessments, further consolidating and enhancing
the quality awareness and capabilities of all staff involved in clinical research.




                                                                                        Annual Report 2025 137
ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND GOVERNANCE REPORT




             Animal Welfare

             Laboratory animals provide indispensable support for pharmaceutical research and development and life
             sciences research. The Company places great emphasis on societal concerns regarding animal welfare,
             strictly complies with the requirements of laws and regulations such as the Guide for the Care and Use of
             Laboratory Animals, and has formulated and strictly implemented operational procedures such as Procedures
             for Establishing Humane Endpoints for Laboratory Animals and Accuracy Control of Drug Administration in
             Animals. In 2025, we introduced the “Operational Procedures for Inspection and Supervision of Laboratory
             Animals” and revised the “Regulations on Space and Holding Capacity of Laboratory Animal Cages” and
             “Procedures for Anesthesia and Analgesia of Animals” to ensure ethical compliance in animal use. The
             Company has also established the Institutional Animal Care and Use Committee (IACUC) to independently
             review and supervise the trial protocol, the use of animals and the operations of facilities, and commit to
             conducting animal experiments responsibly.

             1
              In the practice of scientific research, the Company adheres to the 3R principles, implementing laboratory
             animal welfare throughout all stages including breeding, transportation, experimental design, experimental
             process and post-experiment disposal. The Company has established a fully functional laboratory animal
             management system, which includes two major functional modules: “Ethical Review” and “Animal Welfare
             Ethical Inspection”, achieving traceability and oversight throughout the whole process. In 2025, the Company
             completed a total of 65 reviews of use plans and 2 reviews of animal facilities. Meanwhile, we actively took
             measures to improve animal welfare from multiple aspects: revising anesthesia-related operating procedures by
             adding analgesic content and refining anesthesia protocols; adopting mandibular vein blood collection instead
             of post-anesthesia orbital blood collection to reduce pain during animal blood sampling; enhancing the animal
             housing environment with toys and treats to improve their physiological well-being; and rationally allocating
             existing animal inventory to minimize additional procurement and reduce the overall use of laboratory animals.

             The Company also conducted training for employees involved in animal experiments to ensure each study
             is efficient and accurate, minimizing animal discomfort. In 2025, we conducted four animal welfare training
             sessions focusing on anesthesia and analgesia, ethical review, and inspection supervision, reaching over 30
             participants. These sessions strengthened laboratory personnel’s awareness and practical skills in animal
             welfare, reinforcing ethical compliance safeguards for laboratory animals.

             Pharmacovigilance

             The Company places great importance on drug safety and strictly adheres to laws and regulations such as the
             Drug Administration Law of the People’s Republic of China, the Measures for the Administration of Reporting
             and Monitoring Adverse Drug Reactions, and the Good Pharmacovigilance Practices in its operations. In
             2025, the Company revised its pharmacovigilance system master file and introduced or updated multiple
             pharmacovigilance-related management systems, including the “Operational Procedures for Drug Safety Signal
             Detection and Evaluation”. These efforts continuously strengthened the pharmacovigilance system, enabling full
             life-cycle drug safety management throughout the product lifecycle.




1
     The 3R principles of animal experimentation refer to Replacement, using other methods to replace animal experiments; Reduction,

     animal welfare.

                 Pharmacovigilance Management Measures of Kelun-Biotech


    Clinical Phase

         Formulate differentiated signal detection frequencies, conduct regular signal detection, signal
         validation and assessment on the Company’s active products under research, and formulate
         risk control measures to ensure the safety of participants;
         Collect safety events in the clinical trial protocol. If they meet the criteria for SAE, appropriate
         treatment measures will be taken for the patient;
         Establish a systematic pre-marketing pharmacovigilance system for the collection, processing
         and submission of individual case reports, and conduct pharmacovigilance training and
         assessment for relevant personnel;
         The pharmacovigilance entrustment agreement establishes clear QC and oversight for the
         client's pharmacovigilance activities;
         Establish quality targets for key pre-marketing pharmacovigilance activities and conduct
         monthly statistics on the quality attainment rate.

    Post-marketing Phase

         Set up public email, 400 hotline, official website and other feedback channels to receive
         information on patients’ adverse reactions after medication usage;
         Introduce the professional post-marketing pharmacovigilance database eSafety system;
         Establish quality targets for key post-marketing pharmacovigilance activities and conduct
         monthly statistics on the quality attainment rate.



During the Reporting Period, we conducted 24 signal detections for pre-marketing product candidates,
performed 3 safety signal analyses and 89 cluster signal analyses for Sacituzumab Tirumotecan for Injection,
and have not identified any new safety signals or significant batch or regional clustering. Additionally, we have
established specific risk minimization measures for Trastuzumab Botidotin for Injection. We have developed
separate documents – a Letter to Healthcare Providers and Patient Safety Medication Reminders – for
medical professionals and patients, respectively, aiming to reduce the occurrence of serious ocular damage by
enhancing risk awareness among both healthcare providers and patients.

Product Recall

The Company has established the Product Recall Management Process, stipulating the conditions for product
recall, the handling process of product recall and the methods of product recall disposal, so as to facilitate
thorough investigation, comprehensive assessment and timely recall of pharmaceuticals that may have
quality issues or other safety hazards. The Company conducts annual periodic simulated recall exercises,
proving the effectiveness of the recall management process. In 2025, the simulated recall process showed no
abnormalities.

     2.3.   Supply Chain Management

            A responsible, efficient and green supply chain is crucial for enterprise development. Kelun-Biotech continues to
            improve its supply chain management system, strengthens communication and interaction with suppliers and
            partners, and strives to build an efficient and sustainable supply chain, aiming to achieve mutual benefit and
            win-win with partners.

Supplier Quality Management

The Company has established regulations such as Procurement Management Procedures, Supplier
Management Procedures, Material Supplier Management System, and Clinical Outsourcing Management
Procedures, which clearly stipulate the supplier selection process and supplier conduct standards. Leveraging
control measures such as qualification verification, audit supervision, and performance evaluation, we achieve
dynamic management throughout the supplier lifecycle, ensuring that the quality of their supplied goods
complies with relevant regulations and meets our business requirements.

To optimize supply assurance and deepen supplier communication, the Company has established an objective,
quantitative and scientific supplier assessment mechanism, conducting regular and ad-hoc assessments and
evaluations of suppliers. Each year, the Company conducts performance scoring of major material suppliers,
which account for approximately 80% of the total purchase amount, in terms of quality, price, delivery
capability, service capability, and compliance. Based on the scoring results, a Supplier Performance Evaluation
and Feedback report is issued, requiring suppliers to make targeted improvements and continuously track
the progress of these improvements. In 2025, the Company conducted assessments on 161 major material
suppliers, with excellence and good rates reaching 97.5%. For clinical suppliers, we conduct evaluations
through questionnaires across multiple dimensions including service quality, service efficiency, cost control,
financial management, and compliance and operational risks. In 2025, a total of 166 suppliers were evaluated.

The Company annually engages in communication with suppliers and provides training to help improve the
quality level of suppliers. In 2025, on the material supplier side, the Company organized multiple supplier
quality improvement meetings centered on themes such as vials, infusion and storage bags, culture media,
and deep filtration technologies. We also held numerous comprehensive technical training sessions to deepen
consensus in key areas and drive product and technological advancements. On the CRO side, we organized
communication meetings with key suppliers on a daily, quarterly, semi-annual, and annual basis according to
the type of collaboration, aiming to jointly enhance supply quality.

Supplier Compliance Management

The Company requires employees to adhere to the Purchasing Code of Conduct and the Anti-Corruption
System when conducting purchasing activities, and to complete and sign the Employee Confirmation at least
once a year to ensure that there are no violations of commercial ethics during the procurement process.

For suppliers, the Company requires them to sign the Sunshine Agreement, strictly complying with all anti-
corruption and anti-commercial bribery laws and regulations, declaring that no bribery or other illegal activities
shall occur during the cooperation process. In 2025, all suppliers conducting business with the Company
signed the Sunshine Agreement.

           The Company has continuously strengthened supplier compliance management and conducted compliance
           reviews for suppliers in different segments:

           •      Clinical segment: Send the Compliance Questionnaire to various suppliers in the clinical segment,
                  requiring them to conduct self-inspections for any illegal or non-compliant behavior in their cooperation
                  with Kelun-Biotech. In 2025, the Company sent letters to 116 suppliers and received 111 responses,
                  and no violations of compliance requirements were identified after review. Additionally, the Company
                  conducted inquiries and verifications of suppliers’ creditworthiness and compliance status through
                  channels such as Credit China and the China Sanctions List.

           •      Non-clinical segment: During the annual assessment of suppliers and the admission stage for
                  new suppliers, suppliers are required to fill out the Survey Questionnaire for Supplier Compliance to
                  understand the compliance management situation of suppliers. The questionnaire content includes the
                  establishment of the supplier compliance department, formulation of anti-corruption and anti-bribery,
                  confidentiality, whistleblowing policies, information security, environmental protection, labor and human
                  rights, corporate social responsibility, etc., which can effectively assess the Company’s compliance
                  risk management capabilities. In 2025, we distributed 190 copies of the Supplier Compliance Survey
                  Questionnaire and received 165 responses.

           Sustainable Supply Chain

           ‘The Company focuses on building a sustainable supply chain and has formulated and published its Supplier

           related factors such as business ethics and social responsibility, with a one-vote veto system implemented.

           relevant areas, we will directly disqualify them from supplying.

       During the Reporting Period, we also implemented multiple measures to enhance supply chain stability:

              reagents and detection kits, as well as key production consumables including infusion and storage bags
              and filtration products.

       electric vehicles for the delivery of various materials within the factory premises, achieving 100% transportation
       coverage within the site, reducing greenhouse gas emissions, and promoting the Company’s green operations.

2.4.   Responsible Marketing

       Kelun-Biotech strictly adheres to responsible marketing principles when conducting marketing activities,
       promoting and advertising company products in a manner that complies with business ethics and regulatory
       requirements, and resolutely eliminates any form of false advertising.

       The Company follows the laws and regulations such as the Advertising Law of the People’s Republic of
       China, the Property Law of the People’s Republic of China, and the Consumer Protection Law of the People’s
       Republic of China. During the Reporting Period, we revised the Market Promotion Management Process
       and renamed it the Academic Activity Management Process, comprehensively standardizing promotional
       and non-promotional activities in terms of planning, execution, and promotional materials. Additionally, we
       have formulated and published the Responsible Marketing Policy on our official website, which centers on
       compliance and integrity, incorporates sustainable principles into all marketing stages by safeguarding the rights
       of stakeholders and strengthening governance and oversight throughout the entire process, thereby establishing
       a responsible marketing management system.

       The Company explicitly requires that promotional materials be applicable only to products with approved
       indications or post-marketing products, and the information contained must be clear, accurate, fair, objective
       and verified. The statements regarding product safety must adhere to the principles of fairness and balance and
       reflect the relevant evidence. If the material content involves clinical information, it is necessary to provide the
       minimum information regarding clinical research.

       In terms of responsible marketing audits, our Responsible Marketing Policy details the responsible departments,
       audit scope, and content for responsible marketing audits. In 2025, we conducted audits of academic
       promotion activities, covering all stages and materials of these activities to ensure compliance with regulations.
       Additionally, we conducted on-site inspections of selected distributors and pharmacies to confirm that drug
       storage met quality standards and complied with responsible marketing requirements.

       The Company regularly conducts training for all marketing employees, covering medical knowledge,
       product knowledge, processes and regulations, clearly conveying the Company’s policies and management
       requirements regarding responsible marketing to employees. In 2025, all marketing staff of the Company have
       received responsible marketing training.

3.   DEDICATED TO CONTRIBUTION AND PROMOTING OUR ENTERPRISE VALUE
     3.1.   Win-win Industry Cooperation

            Kelun-Biotech adheres to the concept of collaborative industry development, establishes an innovative
            cooperation platform, builds a technological development collaboration mechanism, and contributes to the
            advancement of the pharmaceutical industry. Meanwhile, we actively participate in academic exchanges,
            promoting technological innovation in the industry to continuously create health value for patients.

            Establishment of Innovation Platform

            In response to the government’s innovation strategy, the Company, leveraging its advantages in innovative
            drug R&D, has taken the lead in establishing the National Engineering Research Center of Targeted Biologics.
            We have established this center in collaboration with the Shanghai Institute of Materia Medica of the Chinese
            Academy of Sciences, China Pharmaceutical University, The First Affiliated Hospital of Zhejiang University,
            Tongji Medical College of Huazhong University of Science & Technology, Second Xiangya Hospital of Central
            South University, Kelun Pharmaceutical, and the National Industrial Innovation Center of Precision Medicine
            to advance industrial innovation. The National Engineering Research Center of Targeted Biologics received
            approval from the National Development and Reform Commission in 2022. The center has been operating
            stably as of the end of the Reporting Period.

            Strengthening Strategic Cooperation

            The Company actively establishes a mutually beneficial strategic cooperation system with enterprises at all
            upstream and downstream stages, conducting in-depth cooperation in areas such as technology research and
            development, market access and product launch, to accelerate resource integration and industrial innovation.

            We have deepened our strategic cooperation with Merck & Co., Inc., forging a collaborative ecosystem of
            “China-originated R&D + global commercialization”. Leveraging our self-developed Sacituzumab Tirumotecan
            (佳泰萊®), we conducted a Phase III clinical trial in combination with Merck’s pembrolizumab (可瑞達®) for
            first-line lung cancer treatment, achieving positive results. This represents the world’s first successful case of
            an ADC (Antibody-Drug Conjugate) combined with immunotherapy, breaking through traditional treatment
            paradigms.

            We have also made our first foray into international collaboration in the autoimmune field, partnering with
            Harbour BioMed and Windward Bio to develop an anti-TSLP monoclonal antibody using an innovative NewCo
            model. The drug is advancing efficiently through clinical trials, conducted simultaneously in China and abroad,
            showcasing differentiated advantages and best-in-class potential. This collaboration sets a benchmark for the
            internationalization of China-originated autoimmune drugs, providing a replicable model for the industry.

            In terms of university-industry collaborative research, we teamed up with the Affiliated Hospital of Southwest
            Medical University under the “university research + enterprise industrialization” model to develop the RDC
            drug SKB107. In 2025, it received clinical trial approval and Phase I trials were initiated. Through full-chain
            collaboration, this drug precisely targets bone metastases in tumors, addressing a critical treatment challenge
            and establishing a benchmark for hospital-enterprise collaborative innovation.

     3.2.   Facilitating Inclusive Healthcare

            Kelun-Biotech regards promoting inclusive healthcare and safeguarding public health interests as an important
            responsibility, with the Board of Directors as the highest supervisory body for inclusive healthcare. The
            Company actively collaborates with multiple parties to explore effective ways to enhance product accessibility
            in order to meet the medical needs of more patients. The Company has formulated and publicly released
            the Fair Pricing Statement, adhering to the principles of value-oriented, tiered pricing, and fair pricing for drug
            pricing, continuously enhancing drug accessibility, actively promoting drug access in developing countries
            and underdeveloped regions, proactively participating in medical assistance and public welfare projects, and
            contributing to the construction of medical and health systems.


                       Case:     Promoting Access to Inclusive Insurance Products

                 In 2025, the Company adhered to the concept of inclusive healthcare and made every effort to
                 advance the inclusion of its products in the Inclusive Insurance programs across various provinces
                 and municipalities nationwide. By the end of 2025, the Company’s products had been successful-
                 ly included in over 50 Inclusive Insurance programs, covering 13 provinces/autonomous regions/-
                 municipalities directly under the central government (including Xinjiang Production and Construc-
                 tion Corps) and 36 prefecture-level cities, benefiting more than 650 million people. The Company
                 actively engaged with local relevant departments, efficiently submitted product information, benefit
                 plans, and implementation proposals to expedite the inclusion of its products in the Inclusive
                 Insurance system, providing patients with high-quality and accessible treatment options, effective-
                 ly reducing residents’ medical burdens, and contributing to the enhancement of universal health
                 security.



                       Case:     Actively engaged in patient assistance programs


                 For its three marketed products (佳泰萊®, 達泰萊®, and 舒泰萊®), the Company has partnered with
                 the Quzhou Medical Health and Community Development Foundation in Zhejiang Province to
                 launch patient care programs. Eligible patients who submit applications can receive charitable drug
                 donations from the company, effectively reducing their medical burden and enhancing their health
                 protection level. For 佳泰萊®, the Company adjusted its free medication program twice in 2025 to
                 expand patient access, demonstrating its commitment to inclusive healthcare.



            Orphan drugs, as special medications for the prevention and treatment of rare diseases, hold significant
            importance. The main product of Kelun-Biotech, RET inhibitor A400, was granted orphan drug designation by
            FDA in 2023. This drug targets the treatment of RET fusion-positive solid tumors, bringing new hope for rare
            disease patient groups and meeting the urgent treatment needs of this special patient population.

            By the end of 2025, three products – Sacituzumab Tirumotecan for injection, Cetuximab N01 Injection,
            and Tagolimab injection – were included in the National Reimbursement Drug List. This move filled gaps in
            basic medical insurance coverage for major diseases such as triple-negative breast cancer and lung cancer,
            benefiting more cancer patients.

4.   PEOPLE ORIENTED AND DEDICATED TO PROGRESS WITH OUR EMPLOYEES
     4.1.   Diversity, Equality and Inclusion

            Kelun-Biotech upholds the principles of diversity and inclusivity, fostering a work environment of equality and
            respect. It respects individual differences among employees and empowers those from diverse backgrounds
            and with varied industry experiences to unleash their talents and realize their personal value.

            Lawful Employment

            The Company strictly adheres to the Labor Law of the People’s Republic of China, the Labor Contract Law
            of the People’s Republic of China, and all relevant national laws and regulations within its operational scope.
            It has formulated the Recruitment Management System, the Employee Diversity and Labor System, and the
            Female Employee Labor Protection Management Regulations to regulate recruitment practices and effectively
            safeguard employees’ legal rights.

            In the recruitment process, the Company ensures that candidates are not unfairly treated due to various factors
            such as race, gender, nationality, religious beliefs, age, marital status, etc., and firmly implements equal pay for
            equal work for male and female employees. In addition, the Company has clearly stipulated in the “Recruitment
            Management System” that employees must meet the national legal working age, and the Human Resources
            Department conducts strict scrutiny of employee identity information during the employment contract signing
            stage. We strictly prohibit the employment of child labor and forced labor practices, and will punish the relevant
            personnel in strict compliance with related requirements once we identify such situation. In addition, the
            Company fully safeguards employees’ right to collective bargaining and signs collective contracts with them,
            covering aspects such as salary and benefits, insurance and welfare, labor safety and hygiene, protection
            of female employees’ rights and interests, and prevention and treatment of occupational diseases in the
            enterprise.

            Talent Attraction and Retention

            Kelun-Biotech regards talent as the cornerstone of development and its core competitiveness. It actively

            School-enterprise Cooperation

            innovation capabilities, and high professional quality, Kelun Pharmaceutical has joined hands with the West

forbidden regulations to ensure that female employees enjoy their lawful rights during menstruation, pregnancy,
and lactation. We have established priority seating for pregnant women on all shuttle buses to ensure the safety
and convenience of commuting for pregnant employees. At the same time, we have established comfortable
and well-equipped nursing rooms to provide a private and convenient space for breastfeeding female
employees. Additionally, we have introduced flexible attendance hours within a specific time range for female
employees, allowing them to arrive and leave work within a 30-minute flexible window, better helping them
balance work and life.

           The Company has established smooth and confidential formal employee grievance procedures, keeping

           subordinates or colleagues, as well as other situations where rights and interests are compromised. During the
           Reporting Period, all grievance incidents related to employee rights were properly handled, with no objections
           from the complainants.

           In addition, the Company insists on conducting regular employee satisfaction surveys annually. In 2025,
           the employee satisfaction survey covered five areas: management, environment, career development, job
           responsibilities and compensation and benefits. A total of 1,891 questionnaires were received. The survey
           results indicated an employee engagement rate of 96% and an overall satisfaction rate of 92%.

The Company continuously improves the employee assessment and promotion system, combines the actual
situation of each department to formulate “Position Descriptions”, clarifies the individual career development
paths of employees, and plays a positive role in the selection, appointment and retention of core talents. In
2025, the Company achieved 100% coverage in employee performance evaluation and assessment. 479
employees achieved career advancement through assessment during the Reporting Period.

The Company respects the career development wishes of employees and has established an internal job
transfer mechanism that allows employees to apply for other positions within the Company, and fully taps into
employee potential. The Company fully considers the job requirements and employee preferences to arrange
internal interviews for employees with job transfer needs. Employees can be reassigned internally upon passing
the assessment. During the Reporting Period, the Company provided internal transfer opportunities to 303
employees, approximately doubling the number from last year, effectively enhancing the organization flexibility.

The Company encourages employees’ continuous development by supporting them in pursuing academic
advancement, applying for professional titles and occupational qualifications, and seeking government talent
awards. In 2025, the Company supported 16 employees in obtaining junior professional titles and 20 in
securing intermediate professional titles through evaluations, while 2 employees were awarded the Excellent
Young Scientists Fund. Additionally, the Company applied for talent incentive subsidies for 91 employees, with
approved amounts exceeding RMB2.8 million.

Employee Empowerment Training

The Company has formulated the Training Management System and the Internal Trainer Management and
Incentive Scheme, requiring the HR department to take the lead in organizing each department to jointly
develop an annual training plan, and to conduct full tracking and supervision of the implementation of training.
By establishing a multi-dimensional training system that integrates online and offline learning with both
general and specialized skills, the Company has created an effective platform for employees to enhance their
capabilities.

We regularly organize corporate culture building activities to strengthen employees’ cultural identity, providing
value-based support for various training initiatives. The elite talent program aims to cultivate high-caliber
talents to build a strong talent pool; the team leader program enhances the capabilities of front-line managers,
reinforcing frontline execution; and the internal trainer system establishes an internal knowledge inheritance
mechanism, empowering various business departments to conduct training independently. Additionally,
each department independently carries out specialized training programs based on its operational needs.
Furthermore, we continuously enrich training content, use innovative teaching methods, enhance follow-up on
training effectiveness, and promote deep integration between training and the needs of business departments.

For new graduate hires, we organized training activities such as production base internships and onboarding
training through the Kelun Pharmaceutical Talent Program Intensive Training Camp, with a total of 46
participants. For managers, we conducted various management capability enhancement training programs to
support employees’ continuous growth.

In 2025, a total of 131 training sessions were conducted at the Company level, with a total of 160,050
participants, a total learning time of 549,523.46 hours, a 100% employee coverage rate, and an average
training duration of 268.72 hours.

                 In 2025, the Company focused on building the

                 comprehensively enhanced the overall management capabilities of team leaders, ensuring the
                 efficient and stable operation of the Company.



     4.4.   Occupational Health and Safety

            Occupational Health and Safety is the foundation of a company’s sustainable development. Kelun-Biotech has
            always prioritized the health and safety of its employees, establishing a robust safety management system to
            solidify the foundation for the company’s stable operations.

            Safe Production

            Safety Production Management System

            The Company strictly follows laws and regulations such as the Safe Production Law of the People’s Republic
            of China and the Occupational Disease Prevention and Control Law of the People’s Republic of China, and
            has formulated safety management systems such as the Safe Production Responsibility System, Management
            System for Factory Safety, Management System for Monitoring and Evaluation of Occupational Disease
            Hazards and Management Procedure for Occupational Health Promotion and Education, clarifying safety
            responsibilities at all levels, and standardizing safety management requirements for factories, workshops,
            facilities, special operations, etc., providing employees with a safe and effective working environment. During
            the Reporting Period, focusing on the core needs of risk prevention and control for special operations, the
            Company revised eight management rules for special operations, including those for temporary electricity safety
            and work-at-height safety, further refining the division of responsibilities and assessment mechanisms.

            The Company establishes a Safety Production Committee. The main responsible person of the enterprise is the
            first responsible person for safe production, and the person in charge of each department is the first responsible
            person for safe production of the department. The Company strictly adheres to the safety production
            principle of “the industry managers must manage safety, the business managers must manage safety, and
            the producers and operators must manage safety”. Safety management work is carried out from three levels:
            company, department, and team, with the person in charge at each level serving as the first responsible
            person.

     In 2025, the Company successfully passed the external audit of the ISO 45001 Occupational

     reached RMB1.6494 million. The Company always adheres to the principle of “safety first,
     prevention foremost, and comprehensive management”, and has set an annual goal of zero
     safety accidents. In 2025, the Company achieved various safety objectives, with no safety

Hidden Risk Screening and Fire Safety

The Company has established a dual preventive mechanism, forming relevant management systems and
clarifying the process of risk classification management and management of hidden hazards. During the
Reporting Period, to enhance safety risk monitoring and early warning response capabilities, we installed visual
surveillance cameras, including newly added image-based infrared intelligent detectors and control hosts,
at high-risk locations within the factory premises. Through 24-hour monitoring, we achieved comprehensive
management and control of fire accident risks.

           To eliminate on-site risks, the Company has strengthened its risk management system by establishing a three-
           tiered inspection mechanism at the post, workshop, and company levels. A closed-loop management model
           has been implemented for hazard identification, registration, and rectification, achieving a 100% rectification rate
           for hazards during the year.

           Additionally, we regularly organize comprehensive emergency evacuation drills and specialized emergency drills
           to enhance the emergency response capabilities of all employees. In 2025, we conducted four company-wide
           specialized emergency drills, with a cumulative participation of 1,324 individuals.


                     Case:      Conducting comprehensive emergency evacuation drill


                In December 2025, to fully implement the fire
                prevention policy of “prevention first, combining
                prevention with firefighting” and enhance the safety
                evacuation awareness and capabilities of all
                employees, the Company organized the 2025
                comprehensive emergency evacuation drill, involving
                a total of 1,016 participants. After the evacuation
                drill, the Company provided practical experience
                activities in four areas: fire extinguishers, fire
                hydrants, simulated escape experience pods, and
                emergency rescue demonstrations, improving
                employees’ emergency response capabilities in the
                event of sudden fires.



           Safety Management of Contractor

           The Company has established the “Contractor Safety Management System”, which clearly defines the full-
           process closed-loop safety management requirements for contractors, including contractor selection, safety
           agreements, site entry management, work preparation, construction safety, and assessment and evaluation.
           Through comprehensive process standardization management, the Company aims to reduce the occurrence
           of safety accidents after contractors begin work on-site.

           In addition, the Company places significant emphasis on providing safety training for contractors and has
           uploaded multiple contractor construction management-related topics, including site entry safety, hot work
           safety and on-site supervisor training, to the online learning center. Each course includes both course learning
           modules and post-course assessments. During the Reporting Period, a cumulative total of 241 contractor
           personnel completed the study and assessment.

          Case:       Special Safety Training for Team Leaders


     To fortify the first line of defense for work safety,

Occupational Health

Due to the particularity of Kelun-Biotech products, the Company has taken a series of stringent measures for
highly active pharmaceutical ingredients to ensure employees’ occupational health. Specific measures include
determination of occupational exposure limits (OEL), classification of occupational exposure bands (OEB),
qualitative risk assessment of the whole process, use of closed equipment and systems, provision of labor
insurance products and training, compliance with waste disposal, and development of special emergency
disposal plans, etc., to continuously optimize employee occupational health protection. The Company also
invites third-party institutions to conduct tightness testing to strictly ensure the health and safety of employees.

The Company has set and achieved an annual goal of zero occupational disease hazard accidents, through
occupational health examinations, labor protection equipment allocation, detection of occupational hazards at
the workplace, and emergency drills, prevent, control, and eliminate occupational hazards to protect employee
health.

In 2025, a total of 556 employees at the Company participated in occupational health examinations, with 0
reported cases of occupational disease diagnosis. Furthermore, the compliance rate of annual occupational
health hazard detection of the Company reached 100%, effectively safeguarding employees’ occupational
health.

5.   FOCUS ON ECOLOGY – CREATE A GREEN FUTURE
     5.1.   Responding to Climate Change

            In the production and operating activities, Kelun-Biotech upholds the concept of green development,
            formulates the “Climate Policy of Kelun-Biotech”, strictly controls the Company’s greenhouse gas emissions,
            and advocates the participation of stakeholders to contribute to the fight against climate change.

            Governance

            The Company integrates climate-related matters into its existing ESG management mechanism and carries
            out climate-related practices from a top-down approach. The Board of Directors regularly reviews climate-
            related policies and performance to ensure the effective execution and oversight of climate-related policies and
            initiatives. Under the guidance of the Board of Directors, the ESG Working Group gradually formulates and
            refines climate-related strategic objectives, while overseeing and reviewing the implementation and execution of
            climate change response actions. Each functional department specifically implements climate-related policies
            and actions. The Board of Directors and the ESG Working Group conduct a review of climate change-related
            matters at least once a year.

            In addition, the Company regularly provides training on climate change to members of the Board of Directors
            and management to continuously enhance the performance capabilities of the Board and climate governance
            bodies. Currently, we have not yet incorporated climate factors into our compensation policies, but we plan to
            include climate-related indicators in the incentive system for directors in the future to strengthen the Board’s
            emphasis on climate change issues.

            Strategy

            Climate Risks and Opportunities

            The Company refers to the “Implementation Guidance for Climate Information Disclosure under the Hong Kong
            Stock Exchange’s Environmental, Social, and Governance Framework” and the recommendations of the Task
            Force on Climate-related Financial Disclosures (TCFD). Combining its own business characteristics, trends
            in relevant domestic and international laws and regulations, and global climate change trends, the Company
            systematically identifies and comprehensively assesses potential climate-related risks and opportunities. During
            the Reporting Period, we identified, analyzed, and evaluated 9 transition risks, 3 physical risks, and 3 climate
            opportunities. We have developed response measures for significant risks to effectively prevent and address
            the impacts of climate change on the Company.

            Based on its business development plans and the climate policy requirements of the regions where it operates,
            the Company has defined short-term (1–5 years), medium-term (6–10 years), and long-term (over 10 years)
            timeframes for addressing climate risks and opportunities.

                    and opportunities.


           Strategy and Decision-making

           The Company implements the low-carbon concept, creates the low-carbon economy, and is committed to
           reducing the adverse impact of the Company’s operations on the environment. We continue to strengthen
           energy management and reduce energy waste while adopting more environmentally friendly equipment and
           more advanced technologies to improve energy utilization efficiency. The Company also actively explores the
           introduction and use of renewable energy to reduce greenhouse gas emissions.

           In response to the increasing frequency of climate-related natural disasters, the Company has established the
           “Special Emergency Response Plan for Natural Disasters” and the “Flood Prevention and Control Emergency
           Plan”, among other systems, and regularly conducts emergency drills to safeguard employees’ health and
           safety as well as protect corporate assets. In 2025, to address extreme torrential rain conditions, the Company
           organized a flood control and emergency response drill in 2025, involving 42 employees.

           Considering the current impact level of climate risks and opportunities, the Company has not yet formulated
           a climate-related transition plan. We will develop relevant plans as necessary, taking into account business
           development conditions and changes in the internal and external environment.

Climate Resilience

Given that Kelun-Biotech’s current skills, capabilities, and resources do not yet meet the requirements for
analysis, a “capability waiver” has been applied for climate scenario analysis. Responding to climate change is a
long-term endeavor, and climate risks and opportunities are influenced by domestic and international situations,
resulting in significant uncertainty regarding their impact on the Company’s business. The Company will
closely monitor policy updates in its operating locations, industry trends, and updates to climate data models,
dynamically adjusting its climate strategy in conjunction with its business strategy to enhance operational
resilience.

Risk Management

We have formulated the “Kelun-Biotech Climate Change Policy” and established a comprehensive climate
risk management process covering all operational areas, integrating climate risks and opportunities into
the Company’s overall risk management process. Compared to the previous fiscal year, our climate risk
management process remains unchanged.

1.       In 2024, the Company’s greenhouse gas emission accounting scope covered production and office facilities in
         Chengdu. In 2025, the Company included the employee dormitories in Chengdu, offices in Guangzhou, Shanghai,
         Beijing and Wuhan, as well as subsidiaries in Hong Kong into the greenhouse gas emission disclosure scope.

2.       Based on the requirements of the “ESG Reporting Code” and the applicability of accounting standards, Scope 1
         and Scope 2 emissions are calculated in accordance with the “Greenhouse Gas Protocol: Corporate Accounting
         and Reporting Standard” (GHG Protocol). Scope 1 emissions primarily originate from the combustion of gasoline
         and diesel in company vehicles, natural gas combustion, and fugitive emissions from air conditioning refrigerants, fire
         extinguishers, septic tanks, and wastewater treatment facilities, and process emissions from the use of carbonates
         and other substances during the production process. Scope 2 emissions mainly consist of purchased electricity
         emissions. The emission factor for purchased electricity in 2023 is calculated using the national average CO2
         emission factor of electricity from the Announcement on the Release of 2023 Carbon Dioxide Emission Factors for
         Electricity issued by the Ministry of Ecology and Environment and the emission factor for Hong Kong issued by the
         CLP Group. Greenhouse gas accounting is presented in terms of CO2 equivalent.

3.       Scope 3 emissions are calculated with reference to the “Greenhouse Gas Protocol: Corporate Value Chain (Scope
         3) Accounting and Reporting Standard”, identifying and organizing emissions based on the principles of materiality
         and data availability. This report covers two categories of Scope 3 emissions: business travel and employee
         commuting. For other categories of Scope 3 emissions, due to the significant cost required to obtain all reasonable
         and substantiated data, a “reasonable data waiver” has been applied.

4.       The GHG emission intensity is calculated based on the sum of emissions in Scope 1 and Scope 2. During the
         Reporting Period, we updated our greenhouse gas emission reduction targets with reference to industry practices
         and recalculated greenhouse gas emission intensity for previous years using the new unit of tons of CO2 equivalent
         per million revenue.

5.       Due to the significant cost required to obtain reasonable and substantiated data for cross-industry and industry-
         specific climate disclosure metrics, a “reasonable information relief” has been applied.


As Kelun – Biotech has not identified any climate-related risks or opportunities in the short to medium term
that are likely to have a significant impact on its financial position, financial performance, and cash flows, and
given the substantial cost required to obtain all reasonable and substantiated data for disclosing the “amount
and percentage of assets or business activities susceptible to climate-related physical and transition risks” and
the “amount and percentage of assets or business activities related to climate-related opportunities”, we have
applied a “reasonable data waiver” for these indicators. During the Reporting Period, Kelun-Biotech’s capital
expenditures, financing, or investments related to climate-related risks and opportunities amounted to 0.

Kelun-Biotech has not implemented internal carbon pricing measures at this stage.

     5.2.   Environmental Management

            Kelun-Biotech strictly adheres to the Environmental Protection Law of the People’s Republic of China, the
            Law of the People’s Republic of China on the Prevention and Control of Atmospheric Pollution, the Law of
            the People’s Republic of China on the Prevention and Control of Water Pollution, and the Law of the People’s
            Republic of China on the Prevention and Control of Environmental Pollution by Solid Waste, as well as other
            laws and regulations, and has formulated environmental-related systems such as the Management Procedure
            for Environmental Protection and the Management System for Waste Collection and Waste Storage, to ensure
            the smooth progress of the Company’s environmental protection work.

            The Company adheres to the principle of “prevention first, combining prevention with control,” clarifies the
            responsibilities of the EHS department in charge of the Company’s environmental management, and sets up a
            reward and punishment mechanism. In response to potential pollution incidents, the Company has formulated a
            comprehensive “Environmental Pollution Incident Emergency Response Plan” and conducted emergency drills
            during the Reporting Period for scenarios such as abnormal operation of waste gas facilities, abnormal effluent
            from the wastewater treatment station, and hazardous waste leakage, effectively enhancing its management
            capabilities in handling emergencies.

            Basic Principles of Environmental Protection Management Procedures of Kelun-Biotech

            •      The director of the EHS department of the Company is responsible for environmental protection work
                   and reports directly to the general manager of the Company;

            •      Focus on the prevention and control of “three wastes” pollution, ensuring the funding, equipment,
                   materials, and manpower for the “three wastes” treatment and comprehensive utilization projects;

            •      Establish regular inspection, maintenance, and post-maintenance acceptance procedures for
                   environmental protection facilities and equipment;

            •      The Company must include environmental protection work as one of the technical and economic
                   indicators for assessment and evaluation;

            •      Anyone who violates the procedures and causes an accident must be held accountable according to
                   the degree of hazard of the accident.

5.3.   Resource Management

       Kelun-Biotech attaches great importance to energy and water resource management, continuously improving
       its resource management system and enhancing resource utilization efficiency.

       Energy Management

                 Case:    ISO 50001 Energy Management System Certification Certificate of Kelun-Biotech


           In accordance with the Law on Energy Conservation of the
           People’ s Republic of China and other laws and
           regulations, the Company has established the Equipment &
           Power Engineering Department as the Energy Management
           Department, formulate the “Energy Inspection System” to
           ensure the effective implementation of energy management.
           In 2025, the Company successfully passed the external
           audit of the ISO 50001 Energy Management System
           certification, with the certification covering 100% of the
           Company’ s production sites.

           Energy Management Measures

           The Company enhances energy efficiency in its R&D and production operations and promotes the achievement
           of low-carbon management goals by focusing on three aspects: improving the energy-saving performance
           of facilities and systems, refining energy-saving management procedures, and implementing energy-saving
           measures.


                                 Energy Management Measures for Kelun-Biotech


               Smart Energy Management Platform

                   By leveraging data acquisition and remote communication technologies from smart meters,
                   and utilizing energy management software, it achieves information-based and visual energy
                   consumption management, providing informational support for energy conservation and
                   consumption reduction efforts.

               Enhancing the Energy-saving Level of Facilities and Systems

                   The design and construction process of the plant strictly complies with the requirements of
                   green buildings. The heat preservation (winter)/cold preservation (summer) performance of the
                   building is improved through material and structural upgrading to reduce energy
                   consumption.
                   Establish the operation facilities, equipment automation and frequency conversion control
                   system, adjust the energy load level flexibly and improve the energy utilization efficiency.
                   Establish an annual comprehensive energy consumption analysis mechanism, and adopt
                   advanced energy-saving technologies (such as HVAC heat pipe control) to reform high-energy
                   consuming equipment to achieve the goal of energy saving and consumption reduction.
                   Replacing the original Roots blower at the sewage treatment station with an air-suspension
                   blower can save 30,660 kWh of electricity annually.
               Refined Management Procedure for Energy Saving

                   Carry out energy saving and consumption reduction patrol work and implement monthly
                   inspection and assessment, and timely correct and rectify problems such as continuous light.
                   Each department shall conduct energy inspection at least once a month, and the Energy
                   Management Department needs to conduct irregular re-inspections or spot checks, promptly
                   provide feedback on issues, and carry out rectifications and archiving.
                   Carry out diversified energy-saving publicity and education to employees.
               Implement Energy-saving Measures

                   Advanced energy-saving schemes in the industry are employed in the new construction and
                   reconstruction projects and energy-saving materials are used.
                   Lighting system modification, including replacement of light-emitting diode (LED) lamps, and
                   use of sound-controlled lamps in public areas.




168 SICHUAN KELUN-BIOTECH BIOPHARMACEUTICAL CO., LTD.
        ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND GOVERNANCE REPORT




         Case:    Implementation of Energy-Saving Project for Air Compressor Control Optimization


    In 2025, addressing the current operational strategy of “two in use and one standby” for the air
    compression system and the excessively high main pipe control pressure, we advanced an
    energy-saving project for air compressor control optimization. The project was implemented in
    two phases: In the first phase, through system parameter optimization, we reduced the main
    pipe control pressure and the average daily loading time of air compressors, achieving an annual
    electricity savings of 43,200 kWh. The second phase, involving automated group control
    retrofitting, is scheduled for implementation in 2026 and is expected to further reduce equipment
    loading operation time, resulting in an annual electricity savings of 135,000 kWh. Through refined
    regulation and technological upgrades, the project has achieved a continuous decline in energy
    consumption.

Water Resource Management

Water Resource Management System and Measures

Based on the Management Procedure for Environmental Protection, the Company continuously improves
water resources management. As a company in the pharmaceutical industry, we focus on the development
and utilization of water cycle technology in high water consumption links such as process water and process
cooling water, increase the water reuse efforts, and achieve efficient utilization of water resources.


          Case:    Implementation of an Injection Water Recycling Project


     Addressing the pain point of significant wastewater generation at the points of use of Water for
     Injection (WFI) in the production process, we completed and commissioned the WFI recycling
     and water-saving project in September 2025. The project involved renovating the drainage pipes
     for WFI in the production area to collect wastewater into a tank, which was then pumped to the
     raw water tank of the pure steam generator in the air conditioning system for recycling as raw
     water for pure steam production. According to actual measurements, the project annually
     recycled and reused 2,340 m³ of WFI, equivalent to saving 3,600 m³ of tap water, achieving dual
     benefits of efficient water resource recycling and cost reduction with increased efficiency.

     5.4.   Emission Management

            Kelun-Biotech continues to strengthen its pollution prevention efforts, focusing on reducing emissions of
            pollutants such as exhaust gases, wastewater and solid waste, to minimize the negative environmental impact
            of its operations.

            Exhaust Gas Emission

            The Company strictly implements the requirements of the “Comprehensive Emission Standards of Air
            Pollutants” and “Emission Standards for Air Pollutants in Pharmaceutical Industry” to ensure compliance with
            emission standards for all types of air pollutants. The Company has installed high efficiency filtration systems in
            the production workshops, and the exhaust gas was treated and discharged by a combination of technologies
            including spray washing, activated carbon adsorption and photooxidation treatment.

            The Company pays great attention to the daily operation management, maintenance, and inspection of
            the waste gas treatment facilities, conducts online monitoring of the concentration of waste gas emissions
            pollutants, and regularly contacts the qualified environmental monitoring units to carry out external monitoring
            of waste gas. The 2025 waste gas emission monitoring results of Kelun-Biotech meet the national emission
            standards, and the concentration of treated waste gas pollutants is far below the standard emission limits.

           Wastewater Discharge

           Through recirculating water usage, the Company reduces the amount of waste water produced in the
           production process, and strictly enforces the requirements of the discharge agreement signed with the
           park sewage treatment plant. After pretreatment process, the production wastewater and the experimental
           wastewater enter the sewage treatment station in the plant area together with the domestic sewage, and
           discharge into the park sewage treatment plant after reaching the standard. Additionally, the Company has
           achieved fully automated and configurable unmanned operation for functions such as influent intake, aeration,
           backflushing, and emergency response at the wastewater treatment station, ensuring operational stability.

Waste Management

The Company strictly follows relevant laws and regulations such as the Law of the People’s Republic of China
on the Prevention and Control of Environmental Pollution by Solid Waste, and formulates the Management
System for Waste Collection and Waste Storage that specifies the full process management requirements for
the collection, storage, transportation, and disposal of general and hazardous waste, reducing the impact on
the environment.

The Company attaches importance to the standardized disposal of wastes, and timely clears and transports
harmless wastes through the sanitation department and waste recycling company. In addition, we have
intensified our efforts in recycling general waste, with over 9 tons of general waste such as waste paper, waste
cardboard, and scrap iron recycled in 2025.


          Case:     Standardized Management Training for Hazardous Waste


     We strictly prohibit the act of mixing general
     wastes and hazardous wastes, and have made
     clear regulations on the collection and storage
     of hazardous wastes, assigning dedicated
     personnel to be responsible for the on-site
     management of the storage warehouse. The
     Company has installed a full set of Internet of
     Things (IoT) equipment, including electronic
     weighbridges, electronic label generation
     devices and video surveillance, in its hazardous
     waste warehouse, and connected it to the
     Sichuan Province Solid Waste Comprehensive Management Platform to achieve full-process
     online management and control of hazardous waste. Meanwhile, the Company conducted
     “Standardized Management Training for Hazardous Waste” annually for departments generating
     hazardous waste, with 1,405 participants in 2025.

INDEPENDENT AUDITOR’
                   S REPORT
(Expressed in RMB unless otherwise indicated)




Independent auditor’s report to the shareholders of
Sichuan Kelun-Biotech Biopharmaceutical Co., Ltd.
(incorporated in the People’s Republic of China with limited liability)

OPINION
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of 四川科倫博泰生物醫藥股份有限公司 (Sichuan Kelun-Biotech
Biopharmaceutical Co., Ltd., the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (together, the “Group”) set out on pages 191 to 249, which
comprises the consolidated statement of financial position as at December 31, 2025, the consolidated statement of profit or
loss, the consolidated statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive income, the consolidated statement of changes
in equity and the consolidated cash flow statement for the year then ended and notes, comprising material accounting policy
information and other explanatory information.

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and fair view of the consolidated financial position of the Group as
at December 31, 2025, and of its consolidated financial performance and its consolidated cash flows for the year then ended
in accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards as issued by the International Accounting Standard Board (the “IASB”) and
have been properly prepared in compliance with the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

BASIS FOR OPINION
We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on Auditing (“HKSAs”) as issued by the Hong Kong
Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”). Our responsibilities under those standards are further described in the
Auditor’s responsibilities for the audit of the consolidated financial statements section of our report. We are independent of
the Group in accordance with the HKICPA’s Code of Ethics for Professional Accountants (“the Code”), as applicable to audits
of financial statements of public interest entities. We have also fulfilled our other ethical responsibilities in accordance with the
Code. We believe that the audit evidence we have obtained is sufficient and appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion.

financial statements of the current period. The matter was addressed in the context of our audit of the consolidated financial
statements as a whole, and in forming our opinion thereon, and we do not provide a separate opinion on this matter.
